Diagrams are generated locally through Gradle from one Structurizr C4 workspace. The portable package includes editable DSL, inline PlantUML code-level definitions, SVG exports and build pins. No public diagram renderer or cloud upload is used.



Current decisions: server and UI are Kotlin/Compose; internal ARC retains arc-protobuf-v1/wire1.0; callback materialization is interface proxy/generic Binder stub/reviewed prebuilt adapter with unsupported abstract classes retained; portable nine-tool bootstrap is default, aliases require explicit group-view negotiation; specialUse is the selected foreground-service candidate with eligibility/distribution gates. Brightness source range remains1..255. C4/Structurizr and Gradle/Markdown/SVG/Marp supersede earlier renderer/document-stack decisions. [S25] supports the documented code-view workflow.


### Unified diagram call paths and lifetimes

The C4 and code-level views use one call path: McpServer asks InvocationPlanner
for a bounded exact plan, AdmissionGate returns an Admission bound to that plan
and current pins, and McpServer calls dispatch(admission). The gate is a canonical
decision service, not a second invocation orchestrator. InvocationPlanner records
actual dispatch/outcome with AuditLogger; an admission decision alone never proves
an effect. McpServer's resource adapter serves owned finite journal reads and
exact-URI subscriptions separately from the finite registration tools/call.

OwnerCommandFacade handles protected changes through the service-owned
OwnerAuthenticationService, using the already specified nonce, exact command,
revision, current foreground activity and single-use30second BiometricPrompt
result. SessionCommandFacade is the bounded unexported route for ordinary session
commands and status. It derives the authenticated principal/context on the service
side and repeats canonical policy; neither an in-process UI reference nor a JSON
principal grants owner authority. Commands that reveal credentials, change grants
or otherwise cross a protected boundary still require OwnerCommandFacade. The
remote controller never receives a capability to approve its own management grant.

BridgeService owns one transport/session registry, policy/authentication services,
repositories, planner, callback broker, groups index and audit sink. ARC has one
registry containing zero or more ArcConnectionSessions. Each connection owns its
bounded input state and frame/log streams; closing it releases its pressed state.
UiDispatcher and selected-root adapters are service-scoped. Cross-view class
stubs name the authoritative view rather than declaring conflicting alternative
APIs; member lists are selected responsibilities, with inherited interface
operations retained. The sender endpoint/token mapping, lease/consent bounds,
death handling and current epochs from the callback chapter remain normative.

The ARC server does not receive a cancellation command for another watch socket.
An incoming Cancel8 gets the retained UNSUPPORTED result; the compatible client
cancels a watch by closing that watch socket. The server closes on peer disconnect,
revocation or bounded shutdown. A terminal error frame is best-effort within its
write deadline; a blocked peer is closed without waiting indefinitely. ARC local
generation/epoch metadata is not silently added to the pinned protobuf wire.

The separate AdbPhoneBackend/AdbConnectionManager owns paired-key protection,
verified endpoint discovery, authenticated ADB connections, reconnect/unsupported
reasons and independent backend health. ArcEngine does not own generic phone
control. BinderHandleProvider selects only a verified supported access profile;
WireContractVerifier binds exact source/generated codec proof to current backend
and interface observations. A descriptor name, API level or caller-provided
`verified` flag is insufficient; unsupported acquisition or codec remains explicit.

Container relationships are explicit summaries rather than the first implied
component edge: the APK reads/writes catalog/configuration/journal/audit storage
and performs authorized SDK/Binder/ADB operations. The reference deployment
shows API35 as Android15, paired same-device adbd, and owner-managed host
loopback forwarding. The workstation-to-phone catalog line is an owner-mediated
SAF file import with validation/staging/atomic activation, not an undeclared remote
deployment service. Documentation rendering is a separate offline build container.
See [Structurizr implied relationships](https://docs.structurizr.com/dsl/cookbook/implied-relationships/)
and [deployment language](https://docs.structurizr.com/dsl/language#deploymentnode).

Architectural interactions remain C4 dynamic models in the same Structurizr DSL.
Their SVGs use Structurizr's supported `plantuml.sequenceDiagram` export mode to
show ordered calls/returns without crossing collaboration labels. This is a
rendering of the C4 model, not a separately maintained interaction model. It is
unrelated to the SVG screen-panel storyboards and Marp UI deck. The documented
export option is [here](https://docs.structurizr.com/export/plantuml).


## Closed lifecycle, transport and owner-boundary contracts

The following contracts fill specific018 omissions; they do not grant permission or claim an Android implementation. Their classes and interaction views are linked below.

### Callback transition and cleanup contract (FR-CB-03/04)

`cleanup_disposition` is one of `not_required`, `confirmed`, `pending`, `cleanup_unknown`; it is independent of delivery state. Delivery is suppressed before any terminal transition. Each transition increments revision atomically with journal/outbox obligations. Event acceptance while registering is bounded and provisional; publish only after a confirmed registration result. A synchronous callback uses its already-validated local response policy even during registration.

| State/event | Next state / reason | Cleanup disposition and action |
| --- | --- | --- |
| registering + confirmed backend result | active | pending only if a later unregister is required; activate finite handle |
| registering + proven failure with no registration | closed / registration_failed | not_required |
| registering + registration_timeout, disconnect or uncertain result | outcome_unknown / registration_timeout | cleanup_unknown; retain exact provisional cleanup pins, never blindly re-register |
| active + target_died (DeathRecipient/service-connection death) | closed / target_died | not_required only if the exact registered endpoint is proven dead; otherwise pending, then cleanup_unknown on failed cleanup |
| active + lease_expired, callback.cancel, credential revocation, contract invalidation | cancelling | pending; suppress payload immediately, enqueue the exact safe unregister |
| cancelling + confirmed unregister or proven endpoint death | closed | confirmed or not_required, respectively |
| cancelling + bounded cleanup timeout/uncertain side effect | outcome_unknown | cleanup_unknown; no automatic replay of uncertain writes |
| any prior live OS registration + process restart | orphaned | cleanup_unknown; never claim old Binder objects are live or restore consumed approval |
| orphaned + supported cleanup confirmation or exact protected-owner acknowledgement | closed | confirmed or cleanup_unknown respectively; acknowledgement is not proof of unregister |
| active + MCP disconnect | active until lease/policy ends | journal persists within quotas; GET SSE disconnect alone is not callback cancellation |

CallbackBroker.onCalleeDied(token) checks exact endpoint/generation before applying target_died. It does not classify every service disconnect as proof that a remote object died. AC-CB-03/04 fixtures must cover timeout-before/after registration, early callbacks, death, lease expiry, revoke/read races, restart and cleanup uncertainty. SCR05.S4 surfaces these terminal reasons and cleanup confidence.

### Negotiated direct event delivery (FR-CB-05)

The standard finite callback handle/resource-journal path is always available to supported baseline clients. Direct events are a separately authenticated HTTP SSE application extension, not JSON-RPC notifications. Server InitializeResult advertises `capabilities.experimental["android.bridge.directEvents.v1"]={version:1,transport:"authenticated_sse",max_event_bytes:65536,replay:"journal_cursor"}` only when enabled. A client opts in through InitializeRequest.capabilities.experimental with that same key/version/transport; the server confirms the intersection in its result. Absent/mismatched negotiation means journal-only mode, explicitly returned as `{delivery_mode:"resource_journal",direct_stream:null}`; initialization itself remains usable.

After confirmed registration, the finite tool result may additionally return a concrete URL under `/v1/subscriptions/{subscription_id}/events` and delivery_mode `direct_sse`. This is a distinct route on the same pinned-TLS listener, never GET /mcp. GET requires the same kind=mcp bearer, active negotiated MCP session header, exact owned subscription, current epochs/lease/read consent and Origin/Host checks. No credential in a URL. Administrative credentials and foreign/unknown subscription IDs fail without existence disclosure. Client opt-in cannot widen policy. The no-TLS reduced profile and clients without negotiation use the baseline journal instead.

DirectStreamNegotiator owns session negotiation; DirectEventEmitter reads already committed EventJournal events and emits SSE `event: callback`, `id: <opaque application journal cursor>`, `data: <one bounded event envelope>`. The cursor is scoped to subscription/stream_epoch/reader policy; here Last-Event-ID is explicitly an application cursor on this custom route, distinct from transport IDs on /mcp. Initial GET may instead provide one `cursor` query value; conflicting cursor and Last-Event-ID are400. Retention expiry returns410 with sanitized oldest cursor and journal fallback instructions before opening SSE. There is no cross-subscription/global order, exactly-once claim or unbounded final tools/call response.

Use at most one direct stream per subscription/session and two per client, with per-stream queue<=64events/256KiB inside existing client/global quotas. Duplicate open returns409. Read from the committed journal; stream disconnect does not delete journal or cancel registration. A slow reader is closed with a best-effort bounded overflow control event containing no lost payload; durable journal retention/gap policy remains authoritative, and reconnection deduplicates by subscription_id+stream_epoch+sequence. Payload over max_event_bytes uses the already authorized bounded-resource reference, never truncates typed data. Revocation/lease expiry suppresses further payload and closes the stream; callback.cancel owns unregister. No stream opens without successful explicit negotiation, and failure returns structured DIRECT_STREAM_UNAVAILABLE plus baseline events_uri. AC-CB-05 tests must verify absent/mismatched negotiation, bearer/session confusion, foreign IDs, duplicate streams, cursor expiry, overflow, reconnect dedup and revoke between enqueue/write.

### Health and local owner capability

One `HealthSnapshot` owns service_state in {WaitingForUnlock,Starting,Paused,StartRequiresOwnerAction,Running,Stopping,Stopped,Error}, desired_state in {running,stopped}, mcp_listener in {absent,binding,ready,error}, adb_backend in {unpaired,offline,connecting,connected,locked,revoked,unsupported}, device_view in {absent,resolving,ready,stale,staging_failed}, and catalog_readiness in {absent,validating,ready,quarantined,capacity_required}. Notification and every screen's non-action STATUS chip use the same snapshot revision. Running means listener initialized; it does not mean ADB/device_view ready. Starting precedes slow work, failure exposes an owner action on SCR10, and desired_state never fabricates actual state. These are display labels for the closed stored enum values, not additional hidden states.

OwnerAuthenticationService is also the OwnerCapabilityIssuer role: after successful protected BiometricPrompt it creates a single-use in-process capability bound to nonce, exact canonical command hash, expected revisions, foreground Activity identity and monotonic expiry<=30seconds. No CryptoObject signature is claimed: the reference uses one trusted process and an unexported facade, so capability storage and consumption remain service-owned memory. OwnerCommandFacade verifies and consumes it at the same serialized commit boundary; CatalogRepository.commitOverride rejects changed command/revision and cannot be called as an alternate UI bypass. Protected windows use FLAG_SECURE and exclude overlay/bridge remote paths. All Ask operations require this flow. Foreign privileged controllers already authorized by adbd remain outside the trust boundary; inability to isolate bridge-dispatched work blocks approval rather than claiming it is safe. Screen text says bridge-dispatched input is paused, never all device input.

### ADB connection and reason-code boundaries

AdbConnectionManager owns one paired same-device ADB identity/session per selected profile. The reference implementation choice is a Kotlin coordinator with a bundled app-private native AOSP ADB pairing/connect client behind a bounded JNI adapter; ABI, build/version/license and Android packaging compatibility are explicit implementation gates, not an installed binary or Kotlin reimplementation claim. It exposes only reviewed fixed operations, not arbitrary shell via a screen grant. Locked means the app cannot unwrap its paired key before user unlock; unlock permits Connecting, never silently repairs or pairs. `verification_strength` CHECK unverified/paired_peer_verified; `identity_verification_method` CHECK none/adb_tls_pairing; connected requires the latter two positive values. Revoke clears the usable key and epoch. Wireless debugging unavailable/offline Wi-Fi returns an explicit environment reason, with no settings change.

Reason envelopes separate `layer` (inventory/applicability/acquisition/authority/codec/transport/policy) from `code` (stable uppercase symbolic enum); arbitrary colon/dot concatenations are not wire codes. Existing specific version codes such as PLATFORM_APPLICABILITY_UNPROVEN remain in that enum. Legacy `backend_unreachable:non_sdk` maps to {layer:acquisition,code:NON_SDK_BLOCKED}; `backend_unreachable:hidl` maps to {layer:acquisition,code:HIDL_BACKEND_UNAVAILABLE}; OS_UNAVAILABLE is authority, not a version claim. Add PACKAGE_NOT_VISIBLE, BIND_PERMISSION_MISSING, ADB_WIFI_UNAVAILABLE and NOT_ON_BRIDGE_CLASSPATH with acquisition/authority/transport/codec emitting layers respectively. The exhaustive versioned code registry preserves old codes and their meanings; unknown future codes remain visible with layer and reason text and deny invocation, never parse a free-text suffix as authority. App package visibility may hide an installed target; this is unknown, not proof of absence. The bridge does not request QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES. Reviewed <queries> manifest declarations are build-time only; an owner may select among supported declared targets but cannot dynamically edit its manifest. Unsupported package visibility stays unknown with PACKAGE_NOT_VISIBLE. Public bindService guarded by a missing permission reports BIND_PERMISSION_MISSING; a catalog SDK member absent from the bridge classloader reports NOT_ON_BRIDGE_CLASSPATH. No permission grant manufactures reachability. [Package visibility](https://developer.android.com/training/package-visibility/declaring) and [ADB](https://developer.android.com/tools/adb) are platform inputs, not universal availability guarantees.

### Hashes, catalog administration and import staging

The administrative entry ETag is exactly the existing cat1 ETag; dv2, group-view and meta1 ETags belong to their separately named representations. Immutable source and SQLite file SHA-256 values hash original bytes. Catalog effective-document hashes use the checked Python canonical profile already implemented by catalog tooling; the exact meta-json-v1 UTF-8/sorted-codepoint/finite-number profile and literal byte vectors in SOURCE-METADATA-CONTRACT.md define cross-language reproduction, including 1 versus1.0 and negative zero. No implicit RFC8785 or Unicode normalization is claimed. Entry source-signature IDs retain the catalog's versioned language-specific signature namespaces: source identities are never silently replaced by a JVM descriptor. A separately proven erased JVM descriptor maps to that identity for dispatch, with collision/ambiguity rejected before activation. Kotlin must reproduce the pinned profile through golden bytes/hashes, not its default JSON serializer; fixtures cover Unicode, escapes, integers, floating exponents, API metadata aliases and conflicting signatures.

An owner enters independently obtained expected SHA-256 and size (or chooses an explicitly pretrusted signed source manifest) before creating an import stage. POST /v1/catalog/import-stages accepts those values and a bounded multipart file stream under the admin window and owner capability. The service copies to a private file, seals its bytes, validates schema/capacity, and returns an opaque staged handle; GET /v1/catalog/import-stages lists only that admin principal/window's sanitized handles. A stage expires at min(created+15minutes, management-window end), or on credential/window revocation; expiry deletes unactivated staging through bounded cleanup. Final import POST consumes the still-sealed handle with fresh exact owner authorization, expected catalog revision and Idempotency-Key. Local SAF follows the same application service without HTTP/admin credentials. Hold a transient SAF read grant only during copying/hash verification, then release it; no persistent URI permission is required. The allowlisted SQLite schema/quarantine contract above applies before any imported content query.

### MCP transport edge cases

An unknown/expired Mcp-Session-Id returns HTTP404 and the client must initialize a new session; a missing required post-initialize session header returns400. Unsupported MCP-Protocol-Version returns400. A missing version header follows the pinned specification's2025-03-26 default for legacy requests; it does not silently negotiate2025-11-25, and incompatible methods fail the protocol gate. The server implements GET SSE; if explicitly disabled it returns405 and resource journal polling remains possible. The client authenticates every connection. A bounded per-session128notification/256KiB queue coalesces URI updates, drops oldest hints under pressure and records a gap counter; payload loss is governed by journal policy, not hint delivery. Each notification is sent to one connection, not broadcast across streams.

Malformed JSON-RPC structure/unknown method/invalid method parameters follow JSON-RPC errors. For tools/call, a structurally valid call with invalid tool arguments follows MCP tool-error semantics (`isError=true`), preserving the pinned tool guidance rather than treating every application validation error as -32602. Transport disconnect alone does not cancel admitted work. A disconnected unary response is retained only as sanitized execution/audit outcome and not replayed on GET SSE; clients use explicit owned status/idempotency recovery. The server's bounded execution deadline is defined by the selected tool schema/caps, not a fictional standard MCP deadline header. Write adapter input may define idempotency_key; duplicates return prior known outcome or DUPLICATE_OPERATION/unknown without re-executing uncertain effects. Subscription IDs use lowercase [a-z0-9-]{1,64}; the exact advertised URI is canonical. Foreign/unknown resources both return resource-not-found without existence distinction. Base events_uri returns the concrete next-read URI; resources/templates/list publishes the separate URI template. [Pinned transport](https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-11-25/basic/transports).

### Remaining bounded platform details and traceability

An exact schema_hash mismatch makes a client binding grant inert and visibly re-grant-required. No automatic rebind is chosen even for graph-only changes; owner preview creates a new exact rule. Capability generation increments only on semantic observation change, not Activity resume/probe time. Entry-point tools are the enumerated bootstrap/wrapper operations; they only restrict canonical grants. Approval state includes Reserved between Approved and Consumed; a crash before confirmed dispatch maps execution to unknown. Fingerprints show at least12hex characters of SHA256(UTF8(credential_id)), with full hash in details; this display is not server TLS identity. Mandatory admission audit failure denies dispatch; optional logs use the serialized writer outside the decision gate and are evicted first.

screen.state displayId is the logical DisplayManager ID on the reference Android15/API35+ floor. `coordinateSpaceVersion` is `display-pixels-v1` for uncropped physical-pixel output or `image-transform-v1` with a complete affine transform to that space. The reviewed platform adapter must prove logical-to-physical display mapping for screencap versus input using the same display snapshot, or report DISPLAY_MAPPING_UNPROVEN; no assumption that both flags accept the same ID. Target bounds come from the same authorized screen.state observation's fixed reviewed window/display query and verified target identity; unavailable/racing evidence rejects target-scoped tap. Whole-display authority is a separate explicit scope, never fallback. Retain at most8capture references/client with least-recent eviction and30second expiry, bounded by the global capture budget. Brightness read observation is {kind:integer,rawValue,withinDocumentedRange} or {kind:absent|unparseable,anomaly,rawDiagnostic?}; raw diagnostics are bounded/sanitized and never a fabricated value.

group_dependencies has an acyclic publication constraint; lifecycle_units pins atomic registration/unregister/type membership; prebuilt_adapters stores ID/version/SHA256/supported methods; cancellations has UNIQUE(client_id,cancellation_id) and sanitized idempotent outcome. connection_profiles rejects profile_id LIKE 'platform:%'; the reserved platform:local route is not a user profile. executions.backend_kind uses the same canonical self_adb/arc_target/native domain as its actual execution backend, never ingress-only external_adb. Legacy avalonia_runtime/executor avalonia rows are migrated explicitly to arc_target after contract verification, not silently interpreted as a managed runtime. runtime fgs_type records the observed last start type and eligibility outcome. Outbox consumers are the callback cleanup worker, committed-journal notification worker and epoch invalidation worker, all idempotent on obligation IDs.

Memory budgets are proposed/unmeasured: Java heap<=min(192MiB,memoryClass), no largeHeap; native allocations<=128MiB; total measured service PSS<=384MiB including graphics attributable to it. The lower page/scratch caps remain sub-budgets. Cache seal is HMAC-SHA256 with a non-exportable AndroidKeyStore key without user-authentication requirement so eligible headless startup can verify it; credential/grant reveal retains fresh owner authentication. Proxy Object methods (equals/hashCode/toString) execute locally and never become callback events. Binder callback stubs implement reserved interface version/hash meta transactions from their pinned contract; missing proof reports unsupported, not guessed values.

FR-DS-01 / TR-DS-01 / AC-DS-01 trace negotiated DirectStreamNegotiator/emitter, fallback, cursor and revocation tests. FR-OWN-02 / TR-OWN-02 / AC-OWN-02 trace OwnerAuthenticationService capability consume/CAS, foreign-input boundary and protected SVG states. FR-LC-02 / TR-LC-02 / AC-LC-02 trace HealthSnapshot, ForegroundStatusNotifier, boot/unlock and callback transition table. FR-ARC-03 / TR-ARC-03 / AC-ARC-03 trace the ArcSession dynamic view, codec/default/cancel tests and exact upstream interoperability gate. These are acceptance plans until executed on Android.

### ARC compatibility defaults and Compose realization

The transport marker arc-protobuf-v1 is upstream, not a coined bridge name: pinned commit19792a52a13a3df342f90bab6b246b547e673919, src/Avalonia.RemoteControl.Protocol/RemoteControlProtocol.cs:31 defines AndroidBridgeTransportProtocol. That file's MajorVersion=1 and MinorVersion=0 form DisplayVersion1.0; upstream package0.8.0 is a distribution version and never substitutes for the wire envelope. [Pinned constants](https://github.com/sharpninja/Avalonia.RemoteControl/blob/19792a52a13a3df342f90bab6b246b547e673919/src/Avalonia.RemoteControl.Protocol/RemoteControlProtocol.cs).

ArcFrameCodec.maxFrameBytes=8388608 applies before allocation; negative/oversize length closes without dispatch, truncated headers/bodies and EOF release the connection. Length zero is permitted by upstream framing and decodes to a default protobuf envelope, which fails version/auth validation; do not change this to a framing-level zero rejection. No empty protocol_version, unknown method0 or absent bearer gains authority. An empty request ID is not upgraded to an authenticated session; the server treats it as invalid request input and produces a bounded sanitized error then closes, with the decoded ID if present. The port compatibility fixtures must record this deliberate invalid-request rejection alongside upstream behavior. Missing end_of_stream defaults false; every actual terminal sets it true explicitly. Incoming Cancel8 returns BRIDGE_STATUS_UNSUPPORTED with matching version/ID and end_of_stream=true, then normal socket close; it never cancels another stream.

The client rejects a response envelope version other than1.0 or mismatching request ID before interpreting status or terminal flag. The server rejects a request envelope version other than1.0 without backend dispatch; capabilities payload protocol_version must also be1.0 and consistent with the envelope. A capabilities-first call is convention, not an invented handshake. Any compatibility exception requires an explicitly versioned profile and golden fixtures; no negotiation guesses. A token is newly generated for each bind, stale marker removed at startup, marker created privately only after successful bind, and marker deleted on orderly stop. Abnormal process death may leave a marker file, but reconnect validates live bind/token/version and never trusts that stale file as readiness.

Compose DIP means dp for the selected root; render_scale is that root's Density.density. Root-origin-relative bounds and logical width/height use dp; PNG dimensions are the actual captured pixel dimensions (rounding from root dp*density is recorded, not assumed exact after crop). Tree, frame and input pin one root generation and transform. ArcInputDispatcher maps pointer/wheel to ordered mouse-source MotionEvents on UiDispatcher, exactly one pointer; maps keys using a committed versioned Avalonia-name-to-Android-key table; text uses supported focused editable semantics set-text, otherwise unsupported. The reviewed table starts with Arrow/Enter/Escape/Tab/Back/Delete/Home/End/Page/letter/digit names and explicit unknown rejection, with platform/IME/layout acceptance fixtures before enabling each mapping. It is a port implementation artifact, not invented numeric key equivalence. No unsupported key becomes an arbitrary Android code. ComposeTargetAdapter exposes only the approved property allowlist and stable node generation; stale node or unsupported focus/property/text returns bounded CommandResult failure, which a client inspects even in an OK envelope. Raw shell or foreign UI controllers remain outside ARC's selected-root input/capture guarantee.

### Design vocabulary crosswalk

| Architecture interface or role | Defining code view / class |
| --- | --- |
| IDeviceCapabilityCollector / IVersionApplicabilityResolver / IResolvedTypeGraphBuilder | server-core-classes DeviceViewResolver owns these bounded internal strategies and immutable DeviceCapabilitySnapshot/ResolvedDeviceView DTOs |
| IDeviceViewStore / catalog projection | server-core-classes CatalogRepository / SqliteCatalogRepository; immutable SQLite staging and CAS activation |
| ICanonicalDispatchPlanner | server-execution-classes InvocationPlanner |
| IWireContractRegistry / IParcelCodec | server-execution-classes WireContractVerifier / CodecRegistry; typed codecs selected by exact proof |
| IGenericBinderTransactionEngine / IBinderTransport | server-execution-classes BinderBackend plus BinderHandleProvider; backend-specific reviewed native boundaries |
| IClientPolicyEvaluator / IAdmissionGate | server-core-classes AdmissionGate; one shared canonical evaluator |
| OwnerCapabilityIssuer | server-core-classes OwnerAuthenticationService; no second issuer or different token contract |
| Local Compose management facade | server-core-classes OwnerCommandFacade for protected mutations; SessionCommandFacade for bounded ordinary session commands |
| ICallbackBroker / IEventJournal / ILocalReplyPolicy | server-execution-classes CallbackBroker / EventJournal / LocalReplyPolicy |
| IFunctionalGroupIndex / IMcpToolViewAdapter | server-core-classes FunctionalGroupIndex / McpServer |
| Internal ARC engine | arc-ui-classes ArcRuntime/router/codec/auth; controller and target detail are explicit reference stubs in their scoped views |

Grouped018 residuals remain tracked individually in the reconciliation ledger. Acceptance evidence is proposed unless a specific retained local build/test receipt confirms it; an architecture table's AC label is never a claim that Android interoperability tests already passed.

                device = component "DeviceViewResolver" "Observed version applicability and coherent binding/type activation" "Kotlin"
                groups = component "FunctionalGroupIndex" "Bounded job discovery and atomic session activation" "Kotlin"
                invoke = component "InvocationPlanner" "Typed generic/raw dispatch, codecs, Binder and ADB adapters" "Kotlin"
                callbacks = component "CallbackBroker" "Registration, bounded journal, local reply and cleanup obligations" "Kotlin"
                arc = component "ArcEngine" "Internal Kotlin port preserving pinned ARC wire/profile; not implemented" "Kotlin / protobuf"
                arcController = component "ArcClientAdapter" "Controller-side session/profile and compatible external-target connection" "Kotlin / protobuf"
                ui = component "Compose management UI" "Screen definitions and selected unprotected ComposeTargetAdapter root; management/protected roots excluded" "Kotlin / Compose"
                ownerCommands = component "OwnerCommandFacade" "Protected owner commands; nonce/revision-bound fresh authentication" "Kotlin"
                sessionCommands = component "SessionCommandFacade" "Bounded unexported session commands; server-derived principal and canonical policy" "Kotlin"
                adb = component "AdbPhoneBackend" "Separate paired ADB connection manager and on-demand phone operations" "Kotlin"
                audit = component "AuditLogger" "Mandatory audit and configurable per-client payload logging" "Kotlin"
            }
            db = container "App-private catalog/configuration" "Catalog snapshots, client/principal mappings, grants/logging configuration, groups/views, journal and audit; secret keys remain in Keystore" "SQLite" {
                tags "Database"
            }
            tooling = container "Offline catalog tooling" "Source acquisition/extraction/schema validation and reference fixtures; no phone operations" "Python"
            documentation = container "Documentation build" "Local model validation and Markdown/SVG/storyboard packaging; no deployed app" "Gradle / Structurizr / Marp"
        }
        owner -> bridge "Manages authorized local bridge settings"
        agent -> bridge "Discovers and invokes authorized Android capabilities" "MCP2025-11-25"
        arcClient -> bridge "Controls approved selected root" "ARC transport arc-protobuf-v1; envelope1.0"
        bridge -> arcTarget "Controls explicitly selected compatible target" "ARC raw loopback TCP; bearer authentication"
        bridge -> android "Uses actual SDK/Binder/paired ADB authority; no universal access"
        sources -> bridge "Supplies pinned offline inventory/protocol inputs"
        owner -> bridge.app "Uses local Compose management and protected approvals"
        agent -> bridge.app "Bounded discovery/invocation/resource reads" "Authenticated loopback MCP Streamable HTTP2025-11-25"
        arcClient -> bridge.app "One request/socket or bounded watch" "arc-protobuf-v1; envelope1.0; authenticated raw loopback TCP"
        bridge.app -> arcTarget "First request/response validates pinned profile; no handshake" "ARC raw TCP; signed4byteBE; envelope1.0; loopback"
        bridge.app -> android "SDK/Binder and separately paired ADB calls, probes and death signals"
        bridge.app -> bridge.db "Reads catalog/policy/groups/views; writes overlays/journal/audit via one serialized writer connection" "App-private SQLite"
        bridge.tooling -> bridge.app "Produces portable catalog release; owner selects SAF document; importer verifies hashes/schema and stages atomic activation" "Owner-mediated file import, not network deployment"
        bridge.documentation -> owner "Produces editable Markdown, SVGs, local HTML and portable ZIP" "Local files only"
        owner -> bridge.app.ui "Manages local settings and reviews protected approvals"

BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT design-c4/architecture/code-diagrams.md SHA256 1c2c28c3f46c6fc3993eb77a1ec6ad471e6681caeea3f8e364a29bd29bb804c5
# C4 code-level detail, using UML class notation

Structurizr has no native class view. These editable inline PlantUML definitions are rendered locally and referenced by image views in workspace.dsl. They are proposed Kotlin classes/interfaces, not implemented Android code. Composition diamonds denote owned lifetime; dashed arrows denote dependency, and quoted labels denote multiplicity. Each block identifies its scoped C4 component. Member lists show selected responsibilities, not every Kotlin member. A reference stub explicitly names its authoritative view; realizations inherit the full interface contract. Local epochs/generations are bridge metadata unless the pinned proto has the corresponding field.

## server-core-classes — bridge.app.transport and server ownership

```plantuml
@startuml
!pragma layout smetana
skinparam defaultFontName DejaVu Sans
skinparam classAttributeIconSize 0
skinparam shadowing false
skinparam backgroundColor white
title Kotlin server core — proposed ownership and contracts
left to right direction
class BridgeService {
  +startDesiredState()
  +stopWithinDeadline()
  +observeReadiness(): StateFlow
}
class McpServer {
  +handlePost(request): Response
  +openNotificationStream(session)
  +readResource(principal, uri, cursor, limits)
}
class DirectStreamNegotiator {
  +negotiate(clientCaps, serverCaps): AgreedProfile
  +requireOwnedSession(principal, session)
}
class DirectEventEmitter {
  +openOwnedCommittedCursor(request)
  +closeOnOverflowRevocationOrLease()
}
McpServer ..> DirectStreamNegotiator
DirectStreamNegotiator ..> DirectEventEmitter
class HealthSnapshot {
  +serviceState
  +mcpListener
  +adbBackend
  +deviceView
  +catalogReadiness
}
class ForegroundStatusNotifier {
  +renderSameSnapshot(health)
}
class BootCompletedReceiver {
  +recordWaitingForUnlock()
  +requestEligibleStart()
}
BridgeService ..> HealthSnapshot
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" ForegroundStatusNotifier
BootCompletedReceiver ..> BridgeService
class McpSessionRegistry {
  +resolveOwnedSession(principal, id)
  +invalidateEpoch(epoch)
}
interface ClientAuthenticator {
  +authenticate(credential): Principal
}
class AdmissionGate {
  +admit(plan, pins): Admission
  +recheck(admission): Decision
}
interface CatalogRepository {
  +streamEffectiveInventory(snapshot)
  +commitOverride(command, expectedRevision, ownerCapability)
  +activateSealedView(pins)
}
class SqliteCatalogRepository
class DeviceViewResolver {
  +resolve(facts, inventory): StagedView
  +activateCAS(expectedView, stagedView)
}
class FunctionalGroupIndex {
  +discover(job, constraints, budget)
  +setActiveCAS(session, desiredSet, etag)
}
class AuditLogger {
  +recordAdmission(principal, pins)
  +recordBoundedOutcome(result, logPolicy)
}
class OwnerCommandFacade {
  +submit(command, ownerCapability, etag)
  +observeImmutableStatus()
}
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" McpServer
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" DeviceViewResolver
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" SqliteCatalogRepository
McpServer "1" *-- "1" McpSessionRegistry
McpServer ..> ClientAuthenticator
McpServer ..> AdmissionGate
McpServer ..> FunctionalGroupIndex
class InvocationPlanner <<reference: server-execution-classes>>
class McpCallbackResourceAdapter <<reference: server-execution-classes>>
McpServer ..> InvocationPlanner : plan then dispatch admitted plan
McpServer ..> McpCallbackResourceAdapter : subscribe / bounded read / URI hint
class OwnerAuthenticationService {
  +issueChallenge(exactCommand, revisions, nonce)
  +consumeFreshBiometricResult(challenge): OwnerCapability
  +verifyAndConsume(capability, command, revisions)
}
class SessionCommandFacade {
  +submitSessionCommand(command, etag)
  +observeOwnedSessionStatus()
}
OwnerCommandFacade ..> OwnerAuthenticationService : verify and consume exact command capability
SqliteCatalogRepository ..> OwnerAuthenticationService : same serialized CAS boundary
SessionCommandFacade ..> AdmissionGate
note bottom of SessionCommandFacade
Service derives owned principal/session context.
No UI or JSON-supplied principal or owner assertion.
Protected transitions use OwnerCommandFacade.
end note
note bottom of OwnerAuthenticationService
Service-owned nonce, exact command/revisions and foreground Activity.
BiometricPrompt strong/device credential, max30s, single use.
UI object or JSON principal is never owner authorization.
end note
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" AdmissionGate
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" ClientAuthenticator
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" FunctionalGroupIndex
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" AuditLogger
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" OwnerCommandFacade
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" OwnerAuthenticationService
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" SessionCommandFacade
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" InvocationPlanner
BridgeService "1" *-- "1" McpCallbackResourceAdapter
AdmissionGate ..> CatalogRepository
AdmissionGate ..> DeviceViewResolver
AdmissionGate ..> AuditLogger
SqliteCatalogRepository ..|> CatalogRepository
DeviceViewResolver ..> CatalogRepository
FunctionalGroupIndex ..> CatalogRepository
FunctionalGroupIndex ..> AdmissionGate
OwnerCommandFacade ..> AdmissionGate
OwnerCommandFacade ..> CatalogRepository
note bottom of OwnerCommandFacade
Unexported bounded local Binder facade.
UI reference is not owner authorization.
end note
@enduml
```

## arc-controller-classes — bridge.app.arcController and local Compose controller UI

```plantuml

No separate handshake. Capabilities-first is a convention.
Validate OK envelope AND CommandResult.succeeded.
Cancel enum8 is not a supported v1 command.

@enduml
```

## server-execution-classes — bridge.app.invoke and bridge.app.callbacks

```plantuml
@startuml
!pragma layout smetana
skinparam defaultFontName DejaVu Sans
skinparam classAttributeIconSize 0
skinparam shadowing false
skinparam backgroundColor white
title Kotlin invocation and callback contracts — proposed
class InvocationPlanner {
  +plan(bindingId, args, callbacks, pins)
  +dispatch(admission): BoundedResult
}
interface BackendAdapter {
  +invoke(verifiedPlan): Outcome
  +observeCapabilityEpoch()
}
class BinderBackend {
  +invoke(verifiedPlan): Outcome
  +observeCapabilityEpoch()
  +transact(verifiedWirePlan)
}
class AdbPhoneBackend {
  +invoke(verifiedPlan): Outcome
  +observeCapabilityEpoch()
  +screenState(scope)
  +tap(scope, coordinates)
}
class AdbConnectionManager {
  +connectVerifiedPairedProfile(profile): ConnectionOrReason
  +observeHealthAndEpoch()
  +closeBoundedCollectors()
}
```

BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT design-c4/build.gradle.kts SHA256 296d268d6fe306c23f6fea0e581e97acd5a65fa4349088313ea189759bcdd266
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.util.Base64

plugins { base }

val toolDir = layout.projectDirectory.dir(".tools")
val diagrams = layout.projectDirectory.dir("docs/diagrams")
val architecture = layout.projectDirectory.dir("architecture")
val pumlJar = toolDir.file("plantuml-1.2025.10.jar")
val cli = providers.environmentVariable("STRUCTURIZR_CLI").orElse("/usr/local/structurizr-cli/structurizr.sh")
val expectedNativeViews = listOf("Context", "Containers", "ServerComponents", "ServerCoreComponents", "ExecutionComponents", "ArcComponents", "Startup", "InvocationFlow", "CallbackFlow", "GroupFlow", "ArcSession", "OwnerTransition", "Deployment")
val expectedCodeViews = listOf("server-core-classes", "server-execution-classes", "arc-ui-classes", "arc-controller-classes", "arc-target-classes")
val expectedUiViews = (1..11).map { "SCR%02d".format(it) } + (1..5).map { "STB%02d".format(it) }

fun sha256(file: File): String {
    val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
    file.inputStream().use { input ->
        val buffer = ByteArray(65536)
        while (true) { val count = input.read(buffer); if (count < 0) break; digest.update(buffer, 0, count) }
    }
    return digest.digest().joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
}
fun javaCommand(): String = File(System.getProperty("java.home"), "bin/java").absolutePath
fun copyVectorWithCanvas(source: File, target: File) {
    val svg = source.readText()
    val end = svg.indexOf('>', svg.indexOf("<svg")) + 1
    check(end > 0) { "Invalid generated SVG root: $source" }
    target.writeText(svg.substring(0, end) + "<rect data-canvas=\"white\" x=\"0\" y=\"0\" width=\"100%\" height=\"100%\" fill=\"#ffffff\"/>" + svg.substring(end))
}

val verifyToolchain by tasks.registering {
    inputs.files("toolchain-lock.json", "renderer-lock.json", "tooling/marp-package-lock.json", pumlJar)
    doLast {
        check(pumlJar.asFile.isFile) { "Pinned PlantUML missing: bootstrap task-local dependencies first" }
        val lock = file("toolchain-lock.json").readText()
        check(lock.contains(sha256(pumlJar.asFile))) { "PlantUML checksum differs from recorded toolchain" }
        val pins = Regex("\"file\":\\s*\"([^\"]+)\".*?\"sha256\":\\s*\"([a-f0-9]{64})\"", RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL).findAll(lock).toList()
        check(pins.size == 5) { "Build-tool checksum manifest incomplete" }
        pins.forEach { pin -> check(sha256(file(pin.groupValues[1])) == pin.groupValues[2]) { "Build-tool checksum mismatch: ${pin.groupValues[1]}" } }
        check(file(cli.get()).isFile) { "Use the documented digest-pinned local Structurizr container" }
        val renderer = file("renderer-lock.json").readText()
        fun pin(name: String): String = Regex("\"$name\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]+)\"").find(renderer)?.groupValues?.get(1) ?: error("Missing renderer pin: $name")
        check(System.getProperty("java.version") == pin("java_version")) { "JDK differs from pinned renderer profile; use documented existing image" }
        val rendererPins = Regex("\"path\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]+)\".*?\"sha256\"\\s*:\\s*\"([a-f0-9]{64})\"", RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL).findAll(renderer).toList()
        check(rendererPins.size == 5) { "Renderer identity lock incomplete" }
        rendererPins.forEach { value ->
            val selected = file(cli.get()).parentFile.resolve(value.groupValues[1])
            check(selected.isFile && sha256(selected) == value.groupValues[2]) { "Structurizr renderer identity mismatch: $selected" }
        }
        check(sha256(file("tooling/marp-package-lock.json")) == pin("marp_lock_sha256")) { "Committed Marp lock differs from pin" }
        val cachedLock = file(".tools/marp/package-lock.json")
        val cachedPackage = file(".tools/marp/node_modules/@marp-team/marp-cli/package.json")
        check(cachedLock.isFile && cachedPackage.isFile) { "Offline Marp cache missing; run documented task-local bootstrap before offline build" }
        check(sha256(cachedLock) == pin("marp_lock_sha256")) { "Cached Marp lock mismatch; bootstrap exact committed lock" }
        check(Regex("\"version\"\\s*:\\s*\"${Regex.escape(pin("marp_version"))}\"").containsMatchIn(cachedPackage.readText())) { "Cached Marp CLI version mismatch" }
        val node = providers.environmentVariable("NODE_BIN").orElse("node").get()
        val version = ProcessBuilder(node, "--version").redirectErrorStream(true).start()
        val versionText = version.inputStream.bufferedReader().readText().trim()
        check(version.waitFor() == 0 && versionText == pin("node_version")) { "Node runtime mismatch/missing: expected ${pin("node_version")}, received $versionText" }
    }
}

val extractCodeViews by tasks.registering {
    inputs.file("architecture/code-diagrams.md")
    outputs.dir(layout.buildDirectory.dir("code-views"))
    doLast {
        val source = file("architecture/code-diagrams.md").readText()
        val blocks = Regex("## ([a-z-]+)[^\\n]*\\n.*?```plantuml\\n(.*?)\\n```", RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL).findAll(source).associate { it.groupValues[1] to it.groupValues[2] }
        check(blocks.keys == expectedCodeViews.toSet()) { "Missing/unexpected editable code view: ${blocks.keys}" }
        val out = layout.buildDirectory.dir("code-views").get().asFile.apply { mkdirs() }
        blocks.forEach { (key, body) -> File(out, "$key.puml").writeText(body + "\n") }
    }
}

val renderCodeViews by tasks.registering {
    dependsOn(verifyToolchain, extractCodeViews)
    inputs.files(fileTree("build/code-views") { include("*.puml") }, pumlJar)
    outputs.files(expectedCodeViews.map { diagrams.file("$it.svg") })
    doLast {
        diagrams.asFile.mkdirs()
        val sources = fileTree("build/code-views") { include("*.puml") }.files.sorted()
        val process = ProcessBuilder(listOf(javaCommand(), "-Djava.awt.headless=true", "-jar", pumlJar.asFile.absolutePath, "-tsvg", "-nometadata", "-charset", "UTF-8") + sources.map { it.absolutePath }).inheritIO().start()
        check(process.waitFor() == 0) { "Code-view rendering failed" }
        expectedCodeViews.forEach { copyVectorWithCanvas(file("build/code-views/$it.svg"), diagrams.file("$it.svg").asFile) }
    }
}

val validateArchitecture by tasks.registering(Exec::class) {
    dependsOn(renderCodeViews)
    inputs.files("architecture/workspace.dsl", "architecture/code-diagrams.md", "toolchain-lock.json")
    inputs.files(expectedCodeViews.map { diagrams.file("$it.svg") })
    commandLine(cli.get(), "validate", "-workspace", architecture.file("workspace.dsl").asFile.absolutePath)
    doLast {
        layout.buildDirectory.file("receipts/architecture-validation.txt").get().asFile.apply { parentFile.mkdirs(); writeText("Structurizr validation exited0\nworkspace_sha256=${sha256(file("architecture/workspace.dsl"))}\n") }
    }
}

val exportArchitecture by tasks.registering(Exec::class) {
    dependsOn(validateArchitecture)
    inputs.file("architecture/workspace.dsl")
    outputs.dir(layout.buildDirectory.dir("structurizr-export"))
    commandLine(cli.get(), "export", "-workspace", architecture.file("workspace.dsl").asFile.absolutePath, "-format", "plantuml", "-output", layout.buildDirectory.dir("structurizr-export").get().asFile.absolutePath)
}

val generateArchitectureDiagrams by tasks.registering {
    dependsOn(exportArchitecture)
    inputs.files(fileTree("build/structurizr-export") { include("*.puml") }, pumlJar)
    outputs.files(expectedNativeViews.map { diagrams.file("structurizr-$it.svg") })
    doLast {
        val sources = fileTree("build/structurizr-export") { include("*.puml") }.files.sorted()
        check(sources.size >= expectedNativeViews.size) { "Structurizr did not export all native C4 views" }
        val process = ProcessBuilder(listOf(javaCommand(), "-Djava.awt.headless=true", "-jar", pumlJar.asFile.absolutePath, "-tsvg", "-nometadata", "-charset", "UTF-8") + sources.map { it.absolutePath }).inheritIO().start()
        check(process.waitFor() == 0) { "C4 export rendering failed" }
        expectedNativeViews.forEach { key ->
            val source = file("build/structurizr-export/structurizr-$key.svg")
            check(source.isFile && source.length() > 100) { "Missing generated C4 SVG: $key" }
            copyVectorWithCanvas(source, diagrams.file(source.name).asFile)
        }
    }
}

val generateUiAssets by tasks.registering(Copy::class) {
    inputs.file("docs/ui-asset-manifest.json")
    from("ui-svg") { include("*.svg") }
    into("docs/screens")
    doFirst {
        val manifestIds = Regex("\"id\"\\s*:\\s*\"((?:SCR|STB)[0-9]+)\"").findAll(file("docs/ui-asset-manifest.json").readText()).map { it.groupValues[1] }.toList()
        check(manifestIds.size == manifestIds.toSet().size && manifestIds.toSet() == expectedUiViews.toSet()) { "UI manifest does not declare the exact required screens/storyboards" }
        check(fileTree("ui-svg") { include("*.svg") }.files.map { it.nameWithoutExtension }.toSet() == expectedUiViews.toSet()) { "Canonical UI source asset missing or unexpected" }
    }
    doLast {
        val exported = fileTree("docs/screens") { include("*.svg") }.files
        check(exported.map { it.nameWithoutExtension }.toSet() == expectedUiViews.toSet()) { "Exported UI assets differ from manifest" }
        exported.forEach { check(sha256(it) == sha256(file("ui-svg/${it.name}"))) { "Edit canonical ui-svg source, not generated copy: $it" } }
    }
}

val renderStoryboards by tasks.registering {
    dependsOn(generateUiAssets)
    inputs.files("storyboards/storyboard.md", ".tools/marp/package-lock.json", fileTree("docs/screens"))
    outputs.file("docs/storyboards.html")
    doLast {
        var source = file("storyboards/storyboard.md").readText()
        source = Regex("!\\[([^]]*)]\\(([^)]+\\.svg)\\)").replace(source) { match ->
            val image = file("storyboards").resolve(match.groupValues[2]).canonicalFile
            check(image.toPath().startsWith(projectDir.canonicalFile.toPath())) { "Storyboard image outside package" }
            "![${match.groupValues[1]}](data:image/svg+xml;base64,${Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(image.readBytes())})"
        }
        val inline = layout.buildDirectory.file("storyboards.inline.md").get().asFile.apply { parentFile.mkdirs(); writeText(source) }
        val node = providers.environmentVariable("NODE_BIN").orElse("node").get()
        val process = ProcessBuilder(node, file(".tools/marp/node_modules/@marp-team/marp-cli/marp-cli.js").absolutePath, inline.absolutePath, "--html", "-o", file("docs/storyboards.html").absolutePath).inheritIO().start()
        val exit = process.waitFor()
        check(exit == 0) { "Pinned Marp slideshow rendering failed (exit=$exit)" }
        check(!Regex("(?:src|href)=[\"']https?://").containsMatchIn(file("docs/storyboards.html").readText())) { "Slideshow contains external assets" }
    }
}

val verifyDesignDocs by tasks.registering {
    dependsOn(generateArchitectureDiagrams, generateUiAssets, renderStoryboards)
    inputs.files(fileTree("docs"), "README.md", "renderer-lock.json", "architecture/workspace.dsl", "architecture/code-diagrams.md", "storyboards/storyboard.md")
    outputs.file(layout.buildDirectory.file("receipts/docs-validation.txt"))
    doLast {
        val svgFiles = fileTree("docs") { include("**/*.svg") }.files
        val expectedSvgPaths = expectedNativeViews.map { "diagrams/structurizr-$it.svg" } + expectedCodeViews.map { "diagrams/$it.svg" } + expectedUiViews.map { "screens/$it.svg" }
        check(svgFiles.map { it.relativeTo(file("docs")).invariantSeparatorsPath }.toSet() == expectedSvgPaths.toSet()) { "Missing or unexpected vector diagram/screen assets" }
        svgFiles.forEach { svg ->
            val text = svg.readText()
            check(text.contains("<svg") && text.contains("<text")) { "SVG must contain vector text: $svg" }
            check(!text.contains("<image", ignoreCase = true) && !text.contains("<script", ignoreCase = true)) { "Raster/script not allowed in vector SVG: $svg" }
        }
        (fileTree("docs") { include("**/*.md") }.files + setOf(file("README.md"), file("storyboards/storyboard.md"), file("architecture/code-diagrams.md"))).forEach { md ->
            val text = md.readText()
            check(!text.contains("```mer" + "maid", ignoreCase = true)) { "Obsolete diagram source in current docs" }
            Regex("!?\\[[^]]*]\\(([^)]+)\\)").findAll(text).forEach { match ->
                val target = match.groupValues[1].substringBefore('#').trim('<', '>')
                if (target.isNotEmpty() && !target.contains("://") && !target.startsWith("data:")) check(md.parentFile.resolve(target).isFile) { "Broken relative link: $target in $md" }
            }
        }
        layout.buildDirectory.file("receipts/docs-validation.txt").get().asFile.apply { parentFile.mkdirs(); writeText("Vector SVGs=${svgFiles.size}; Markdown relative links valid; no raster substitutions; no public rendering\n") }
    }
}

val generateArtifactManifest by tasks.registering {
    dependsOn(verifyDesignDocs)

    outputs.file("docs/checksums.sha256")
    doLast {
        val files = (fileTree("docs") { exclude("checksums.sha256") }.files + fileTree("architecture").files + fileTree("ui-svg").files + fileTree("storyboards").files + fileTree("gradle").files + fileTree("tooling").files + listOf("README.md", "build.gradle.kts", "settings.gradle.kts", "gradle.properties", "gradlew", "gradlew.bat", "toolchain-lock.json", "renderer-lock.json").map { file(it) }).filter { !it.path.contains("__pycache__") }.sortedBy { it.relativeTo(projectDir).invariantSeparatorsPath }
        file("docs/checksums.sha256").writeText(files.joinToString("\n", postfix="\n") { "${sha256(it)}  ${it.relativeTo(projectDir).invariantSeparatorsPath}" })
    }
}

    from("tooling") { into("tooling"); exclude("**/__pycache__/**") }
    from("build/receipts") { into("validation") }
    from(listOf("README.md", "build.gradle.kts", "settings.gradle.kts", "gradle.properties", "gradlew", "gradlew.bat", "toolchain-lock.json", "renderer-lock.json"))
}

BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT design-c4/README.md SHA256 b0d4366094e18ff6dbb326f3f14b91944378c6cdabce6d4f1d54fe2ee6160911
# Android MCP design documentation

Canonical design: [Markdown](<docs/Android MCP Bridge Design.md>). [Screen definitions](docs/ui-screens-and-storyboards.md). [Storyboard deck](storyboards/storyboard.md). Design and offline tooling only: no deployed Kotlin Android app, phone actions or public service.

Use Gradle8.14.3 with Java21, the exact StructurizrCLI2025.11.09 image digest in toolchain-lock.json, PlantUML1.2025.10 and MarpCLI4.2.3. Existing local image supplies Java/Graphviz. Node26.8.2 was used for local Marp HTML. Dependencies reside under .tools and .gradle-home; no host installation is needed. The Gradle wrapper pins distribution SHA256. Keep these folders out of the portable archive; bootstrap verified dependencies into task-local caches first. The wrapper does not make a fresh cache magically offline-ready.

## Targets

Run ./gradlew --offline validateArchitecture generateArchitectureDiagrams generateUiAssets renderStoryboards verifyDesignDocs packageDesignDocs. The same invocation is used inside the pinned container with only this docs project mounted, NODE_BIN selecting a compatible existing read-only Node runtime, network disabled and no exposed ports. STRUCTURIZR_CLI defaults to the existing image's /usr/local/structurizr-cli/structurizr.sh. The README records the actual invocation and receipts once complete.

validateArchitecture depends on locally rendered code-view images and fails on invalid DSL. generateArchitectureDiagrams exports native C4 views through Structurizr then renders SVGs locally; image views scope the editable inline PlantUML class detail. generateUiAssets copies editable vector screen/storyboard source. renderStoryboards embeds SVG assets into a finite local Marp HTML deck. verifyDesignDocs fails on missing outputs, broken Markdown links or raster/script substitutions. packageDesignDocs creates a deterministic archive with only current docs/SVG/source/build configuration and validation receipts. No diagram is sent to a public renderer and no Structurizr workspace is uploaded.

## Tooling and license

The internal ARC port's pinned source and exact field/enum contract are documented in the design. MIT package metadata was verified upstream; authoritative notices remain a pre-distribution gate for copied/translated schema/code. This package contains the derived design contract, not the upstream source archive or a Kotlin port. Gradle/Structurizr/PlantUML/Marp are build dependencies with their own licenses; the archive includes configuration and wrapper provenance, not downloaded distributions or node_modules.

## Bootstrap and actual isolated build

On a machine with Python3/npm and the pinned existing renderer image available, run python3 tooling/bootstrap.py. It downloads only checksum-recorded Gradle/PlantUML task dependencies and performs npm ci with the included Marp integrity lock, without lifecycle scripts or global installation. Java21/Graphviz come from the documented image; this is separate from any Android app installation. Offline use requires a populated wrapper distribution and Marp cache.

The validated environment uses the official Gradle wrapper in the existing digest-pinned Structurizr container, user1000, network none, no exposed ports, task directory mounted at /workspace and an existing compatible Node binary at /tool-node/node. Node26.8.2 requires libatomic.so.1; an existing host copy is mounted read-only in that ephemeral container, its hash retained in toolchain-lock.json. This is an environment dependency, not a hidden package installation. The strict renderer-lock.json profile checks the exact JDK21.0.8, Node26.8.2, renderer jar hashes and committed/cached Marp lock. An environment mismatch fails rather than claiming byte reproducibility. Alternate runtimes require an explicitly reviewed new lock/profile. No task starts a public diagram server.

Execution and model/docs validation receipts are in validation/ in the archive. The portable ZIP omits .tools, .gradle-home, all source catalog data, reviewer receipts, old diagram outputs, Word/PDF and raster mockups.

Canonical UI source is ui-svg/*.svg; generateUiAssets verifies every manifest ID and copies identical bytes to docs/screens. Current package has34 unique docs SVGs:13 C4 views,5 class views,11 screens and5 storyboards. No Android application is generated.

END ARTIFACT

# Kotlin Compose SVG screen definitions and storyboards

Canonical editable sources are `ui-svg/`; Gradle copies them to `docs/screens/`. Edit source SVG and its manifest together. Reference units map to Compose dp; action/navigation bounds are48dp. Narrow windows use the same IDs in a single pane. These are screen definitions, not an implemented UI.

## SCR01 — Dashboard

FR-KT-01 / FR-KT-03. SCR01.C01 = Start / Stop service; C02 = Open subscriptions; C03 = navigation. SCR09 is modal with navigation locked. SCR02.S1 offers Pair locally -> SCR11. States S1–S3 are explicit alternatives; S0 is the pictured example. SCR07.C04 is input, enabled only in the connected and authorized state.

![SCR01](screens/SCR01.svg)

## SCR02 — Clients and permissions

FR-KT-02 / canonical client grants. SCR02.C01 = Edit exact grants; C02 = Revoke credential; C03 = navigation. SCR09 is modal with navigation locked. SCR02.S1 offers Pair locally -> SCR11. States S1–S3 are explicit alternatives; S0 is the pictured example. SCR07.C04 is input, enabled only in the connected and authorized state.

![SCR02](screens/SCR02.svg)

## SCR03 — Full binding catalog

All bindings / FR-DEV-01. SCR03.C01 = Inspect evidence; C02 = Edit local override; C03 = navigation. SCR09 is modal with navigation locked. SCR02.S1 offers Pair locally -> SCR11. States S1–S3 are explicit alternatives; S0 is the pictured example. SCR07.C04 is input, enabled only in the connected and authorized state.

![SCR03](screens/SCR03.svg)

## SCR04 — Groups for a job

FR-GRP-01 / 02 / 03. SCR04.C01 = Inspect group summary; C02 = Save group mapping; C03 = navigation. SCR09 is modal with navigation locked. SCR02.S1 offers Pair locally -> SCR11. ![SCR08](screens/SCR08.svg)

FR-ARC-04 / canonical Ask approval. SCR09.C01 = Authenticate and approve; C02 = Deny / dismiss; C03 = navigation. SCR09 is modal with navigation locked. SCR02.S1 offers Pair locally -> SCR11. States S1–S3 are explicit alternatives; S0 is the pictured example. SCR07.C04 is input, enabled only in the connected and authorized state.

FR-KT-03 / no fabricated readiness. SCR10.C01 = Review prerequisites; C02 = Return to dashboard; C03 = navigation. SCR09 is modal with navigation locked. SCR02.S1 offers Pair locally -> SCR11. States S1–S3 are explicit alternatives; S0 is the pictured example. SCR07.C04 is input, enabled only in the connected and authorized state.

![SCR10](screens/SCR10.svg)

## SCR11 — Client pairing

FR-KT-02 / canonical client identity. SCR11.C01 = Generate pairing challenge; C02 = Cancel pairing; C03 = navigation. SCR09 is modal with navigation locked. SCR02.S1 offers Pair locally -> SCR11. States S1–S3 are explicit alternatives; S0 is the pictured example. SCR07.C04 is input, enabled only in the connected and authorized state.

![SCR11](screens/SCR11.svg)

## STB01 — Inspect an agent session and stop a callback

The agent owns MCP discovery/activation/registration/resource reads. The owner UI observes scoped metadata; administrative cancellation uses protected owner authority, not client impersonation.

![STB01](screens/STB01.svg)

## STB02 — ARC connect, inspect, control and disconnect

Existing ARC v1 bytes remain unchanged. Target operation controls are capability/policy gated; protected local owner surfaces are never an ARC root.

![STB02](screens/STB02.svg)

## STB03 — Edit configuration and observe real startup

SCR01 is the success surface; SCR10 is the failure branch. UI recreation never implies foreground-service shutdown.

![STB03](screens/STB03.svg)

## STB04 — Pair, review grants and revoke a client

SCR02.S1 routes Pair locally to SCR11. Grant edits and revocation use the exact owner flow; display names or group interests grant nothing.

![STB04](screens/STB04.svg)

## STB05 — Override catalog metadata and export logs

Local overrides cannot manufacture imported availability or grants. Export is owner-scoped, redacted and separate from mandatory audit retention.

![STB05](screens/STB05.svg)

[Editable Marp deck](../storyboards/storyboard.md) · [Self-contained slideshow](storyboards.html)

Every SCRxx.STATUS chip is a read-only AppBar projection of the same HealthSnapshot revision as the Android foreground notification; it is not an approval control. SCR05.S3/S4 show target death, lease expiry, orphan/revoke and confirmed versus unknown cleanup. Screen/card values are illustrative, not a running application.

BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT catalog/SOURCE-METADATA-CONTRACT.md SHA256 42d41a92934fe68d608175a9f231a89b25942e1154f42a60a117efc600f3d29e
# Source metadata integration contract 1.0.0

This document specifies offline catalog production and a SQLite reference oracle.
It does not implement a Kotlin service, HTTP listener, callback runtime, group
activation, device probe or verified wire codec. Current design remains separate.

## Canonical representation

`Entry.extensions.source_contract_metadata` is a `SourceContractMetadataSet`:
`schema_version`, `entry_id`, and nonempty `variants`. Each variant is a
`SourceContractMetadata` envelope tied to one immutable declaration occurrence.
The optional addition keeps the Entry 1.1 envelope; the embedded contract has its
own 1.0.0 version and strict known fields. Unknown later contract versions must
remain preserved as opaque import evidence, explicitly unsupported for runtime
interpretation; do not silently normalize them into this version.

An exact variant carries source IDs and the SHA-256 of its original
`binding_declarations.document_json` bytes. `attach_occurrences` verifies those
bytes, identity and provenance before generating facts. No one-way flags, inherited
type shapes or argument roles are borrowed from the aggregate effective Entry.
Without exact occurrence context, `aggregate-unresolved` is retained with a
reason, unknown reply mode and no inferred inheritance. Duplicate exact contexts
are rejected. The helper is a build hook before effective Entry serialization,
record hashing and export; it does not mutate original declaration rows.

Source IDs depend on processing provenance. Join evidence using verified
`(repository, revision, path, sha256)` against the current build's source registry,
then bind its new source ID. Never carry release011 source IDs into a new build.
Do not match on a basename, owner name, descriptor literal or API level alone.
The importer must reject a join whose byte hash, repository or revision differs.
`evidence_lookup` is trusted importer code, not a request-supplied callback.

## Evidence and uncertainty

`interface_evidence` preserves source-interface-evidence/1 DTOs, ordered published
hash candidates and Soong/APEX availability selectors. A build module is not an
installed Android module identity. Published source candidates do not prove a
runtime interface hash, generated ABI equivalence or installed version range.
`hidl_source_constraints` preserves exact source-hash predicates OR explicit
unknown reasons. Translate a validated predicate into a DeviceResolution Rule
only through its defined clause; do not put the whole proof envelope into a Rule.
No platform API range is inferred from HIDL/AIDL version numbers.

`callback_facts` preserves type disposition, endpoint method, parameter and
lifecycle-pair candidates. AIDL reply mode is one-way if either explicit or
interface-inherited flag is true, synchronous only if both are explicitly false,
and otherwise unknown. HIDL uses its own explicit one-way flag. Other source
languages remain unknown here: Java `void` is not Binder one-way proof. Null
parameter direction stays null. Name heuristics never prove callback direction,
registration, cleanup, threading, return policy or stream semantics.

`functional_facts` preserves annotation facts and namespace search labels. Every
unclassified entry stays searchable; package names do not automatically become
functional groups. Source facts do not populate proven group memberships.

Native evidence includes ABI records, resolved/unresolved source-local type
references, function-pointer parameter candidates and five supported descriptor
declaration families. Preserve raw/computed/unclosed token forms, unresolved build
conditions and unknown runtime registration. ABI local keys/evidence IDs are not
MCP binding IDs. The evidence wrapper fixes confidence to `structural-only` and
runtime observation, wire proof and authority to false. Global native coverage is
not proven by these supported forms. Unmatched evidence remains in a schema-valid
sidecar indexed by source identity, not discarded for lack of an Entry join.

## Declarative resources and owner CRUD

`CallbackDescriptor` and `FunctionalGroup` are separate versioned collections,
not new Entry kinds and not inferred runtime grants. Their exact source contract
hashes pin catalog entities; final device-resolved transitive schema hashes are
computed later. Candidate/unknown callback roles require a reason; proven roles
and cleanup require evidence. Abstract classes require a reviewed, versioned,
hashed prebuilt adapter or explicit unsupported status. No runtime subclassing
or registration follows from saving a descriptor.

Materialization is none for a proven non-callback, unknown with a reason for an
unknown role, and explicitly unsupported when a class needs an absent reviewed
adapter. Concrete non-final class subclassing is distinct from abstract classes.
A candidate's known shape is not proof of callback role or registration.

Groups contain at most 128 explicit members, required types, pinned dependencies
and atomic registration/cleanup units. Unassigned catalog entries remain available
to discovery. All referenced catalog IDs and hashes must be checked against a
trusted current catalog index. Missing index/reference, wrong hash, non-type type
reference, unknown dependency or cyclic group graph is 422. Dependency revisions
remain pinned; changing a dependency invalidates activation of dependents until a
new coherent revision is selected. Administrative `active` does not mean invocable.

The offline `MetadataCatalog` implements immutable base imports and revision
history, local overlays, imported suppression, local tombstones and explicit
conflict rebasing. Owner/importer capabilities are trusted fixture inputs, never
JSON request fields. A future host must enforce protected owner authorization
and read visibility before invoking it; it is not an authentication provider.

Create uses PUT at the ITEM URI with `If-None-Match: *`: success201, existing
identity412. No conditional POST targets an already-existing collection.
Replace PUT uses `If-Match`; exactly one create/replace precondition is allowed.
DELETE requires an exact strong ETag. Missing precondition is 428, malformed is 400,
stale is 412, unauthorized owner is 403, missing identity is 404, invalid document
or reference is 422. PUT replaces the whole declarative document, never PATCH.
DELETE preserves history and returns the suppressed/deleted representation. A
repeat DELETE with the current ETag is idempotent. PUT of a tombstone restores
it. A conflicting newer import requires explicit resolution against its current
base hash; merely sending a fresh ETag does not resolve an overlay conflict.
Retrying a stale conditional DELETE can return412 without further effect; HTTP
idempotence concerns intended effects, not identical responses. GET current state
before a fresh intent. Conflict resolution is mutation-body
`resolution:{action:"rebase"|"retain-local",expected_base_hash:hash|null}`;
the action must match imported-base versus local-only conflict semantics.

`"meta1:<generation>:<kind>:<sha256(resource_id UTF8)>:<base_hash|local>:<revision>:<state>:<content_hash>"`
is the strong metadata ETag. A successful head change increments the global
metadata generation atomically, invalidating all old metadata cursors and ETags.
This is conservative metadata-view CAS: unrelated writes can cause a false
conflict. The public representation contains its current ETag and therefore also
changes. No per-resource edit independence or high-write-rate pagination is
claimed. Clients reload/rebase exact intent; bounded listing retries eventually
report busy/restart instead of retrying indefinitely. The closed ETag state set
is active/suppressed/deleted/conflict.

Content encoding is `meta-json-v1`, mapped to document schema_version1.0.0:
CPython3.14 JSON encoder semantics with `sort_keys=True`, `separators=(',',':')`,
`ensure_ascii=False`, `allow_nan=False`, then strict UTF-8. Keys sort by Unicode
scalar/code-point order, not UTF-16 code units; strings keep their code points
without normalization. Quote/backslash and control characters use the encoder's
standard JSON escapes; slash and valid non-ASCII are unescaped. Integers render
exact decimal; finite binary64 floats use its shortest-roundtrip repr, including
integral-float decimal points, negative zero and exponent spelling: `1` differs
from `1.0`; examples are `-0.0`, `1e-07`, `1e+20`. Non-finite numbers, lone
surrogates and non-JSON keys are rejected. The offline digest and literal-byte
golden vectors are normative; a Kotlin encoder must pass integer/float/Unicode/
escaping fixtures before interoperability. This is not a JCS claim. Original
source/declaration hashes verify original bytes without re-serialization. A
canonicalization change requires a versioned migration.
Base/import hashes and local history cannot be edited or
deleted. SQL null cannot satisfy schema-version or identity checks. SQLite FK,
JSON checks and immutability triggers supplement full JSON Schema/semantic
validation; SQL alone does not prove every descriptor field or reference.

Read/list/history HTTP routes are specifications in OpenAPI, not a deployed API.
Metadata list cursors must pin authenticated reader, collection, registry and
metadata generation; bounded keyset pagination, maximum 100 resources, rejects
cross-reader/stale cursors with 410. Retrying the same cursor under unchanged pins
is allowed for a lost response; it is not consume-once. Non-active/history visibility is
owner-only. Source metadata reads follow Entry read visibility; absence is an
explicit `not-produced` result with reason, never an empty proof of no callbacks.
This offline oracle currently exercises generation/reader cursor invalidation;
HTTP serialization, opaque authenticated cursors and middleware remain host work.
The trusted index is the currently published sealed catalog, never a staged
import. Later suppression or contract/type changes leave historical resources
inspectable but make activation fail stale/unavailable until an explicit coherent
revision is validated. A stored descriptor or group never grants authority.

HTTP semantics follow [RFC9110](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9110.html): opaque
strong ETags, item-target creation preconditions and idempotent intended effects.
The unshipped OpenAPI1.5 collection-create draft is superseded by1.6; source DTO
schema1.0 stays separate. No HTTP host is implemented by the reference.

## Integration and validation

1. Merge the exact canonical schemas/helpers into an isolated source candidate.
   Preserve the source owner's newer parser changes. Copy helper hashes exactly.
2. Add the occurrence hook before `content=js(doc); digest=sha(content)`. Query
   original declaration JSON plus its stored hash; do not use projected or
   aggregate JSON as the immutable hash input. A trusted occurrence projection
   may supply additional source-specific facts only with separately bound proof.
3. Retain source sidecars for unjoined native/interface evidence. Unknown records
   must remain visible, including 173 unresolved HIDL constraints in the tested
   export. Do not fabricate runtime Rule predicates from source selectors.
4. Validate Entry and metadata, re-hash the effective document and regenerate all
   catalogs/SQLite/export manifests together. Keep old published releases immutable.
5. Run normal and optimized suites, real-export structural validation and full
   publication/schema/FK/hash reconciliation on the rebuilt candidate. Exact
   producer/contract hashes must accompany the next review snapshot.

Reference fixtures cover the actual builder occurrence -> metadata -> Entry
schema -> new effective hash path, malformed immutable hash rejection,
unknown preservation, identity checks, source/authority separation, CRUD/CAS,
immutable imports/history, rollback, reference pins and dependency/lifecycle
constraints. They do not demonstrate Kotlin runtime callbacks or production
semantic completeness. Combined review017 intentionally uses the earlier frozen
baseline; these later changes require a fresh review before acceptance.

END ARTIFACT

BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT arc-upstream/19792a52a13a3df342f90bab6b246b547e673919/src/Avalonia.RemoteControl.Protocol/RemoteControlProtocol.cs SHA256 f39ffcf2bf8c0395ed1707a6ed3aac523358414ea4eb0b545c070c3f11ad2f21
namespace Avalonia.RemoteControl.Protocol;

/// <summary>
/// Defines protocol-level constants shared by server and client packages.
/// </summary>
public static class RemoteControlProtocol
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Current major protocol version.
    /// </summary>
    public const int MajorVersion = 1;

    /// <summary>
    /// Current minor protocol version.
    /// </summary>
    public const int MinorVersion = 0;

    /// <summary>
    /// Default debuggee endpoint port used by local and ADB-forwarded sessions.
    /// </summary>
    public const int DefaultPort = 47100;

    /// <summary>
    /// Marker protocol value for the desktop-facing gRPC transport.
    /// </summary>
    public const string GrpcTransportProtocol = "grpc";

    /// <summary>
    /// Marker protocol value for the Android-compatible protobuf bridge transport.
    /// </summary>
    public const string AndroidBridgeTransportProtocol = "arc-protobuf-v1";

    /// <summary>
    /// Logger category used for transport keepalive entries on otherwise idle log streams.
    /// </summary>
    public const string LogStreamKeepAliveCategory = "Avalonia.RemoteControl.LogStreamKeepAlive";

    /// <summary>
    /// Current protocol version rendered as a display string.
    /// </summary>
    public static string DisplayVersion => $"{MajorVersion}.{MinorVersion}";
}
