BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT arc-upstream/19792a52a13a3df342f90bab6b246b547e673919/src/Avalonia.RemoteControl.Protocol/BridgeFrameCodec.cs SHA256 fc0f60ddcdbab4c51804f6620f064064c0b7acf6331507c8207e6be390476e99
using System.Buffers.Binary;
using Google.Protobuf;

namespace Avalonia.RemoteControl.Protocol;

/// <summary>
/// Encodes and decodes length-prefixed protobuf bridge frames.
/// </summary>
public static class BridgeFrameCodec
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Number of bytes in the big-endian frame length prefix.
    /// </summary>
    public const int LengthPrefixByteCount = 4;

    /// <summary>
    /// Default maximum bridge frame payload size.
    /// </summary>
    public const int DefaultMaxFrameLength = 8 * 1024 * 1024;

    /// <summary>
    /// Encodes a protobuf message as a length-prefixed bridge frame.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="message">Message to encode.</param>
    /// <returns>Length-prefixed frame bytes.</returns>
    public static byte[] Encode(IMessage message)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(message);

        var payload = message.ToByteArray();
        var frame = new byte[LengthPrefixByteCount + payload.Length];
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt32BigEndian(frame.AsSpan(0, LengthPrefixByteCount), payload.Length);
        payload.CopyTo(frame.AsSpan(LengthPrefixByteCount));
        return frame;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Decodes a complete length-prefixed bridge frame.
    /// </summary>
    /// <typeparam name="T">Message type.</typeparam>
    /// <param name="frame">Complete frame bytes.</param>
    /// <param name="parser">Message parser.</param>
    /// <param name="maxFrameLength">Maximum accepted payload size.</param>
    /// <returns>Decoded protobuf message.</returns>
    public static T Decode<T>(
        ReadOnlySpan<byte> frame,
        MessageParser<T> parser,
        int maxFrameLength = DefaultMaxFrameLength)
        where T : IMessage<T>
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(parser);

        if (frame.Length < LengthPrefixByteCount)
        {
            throw new InvalidDataException("Bridge frame did not contain a complete length prefix.");
        }

        var length = BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt32BigEndian(frame[..LengthPrefixByteCount]);
        ValidateLength(length, maxFrameLength);

        if (frame.Length - LengthPrefixByteCount != length)
        {
            throw new InvalidDataException("Bridge frame length did not match the payload length.");
        }

        return parser.ParseFrom(frame[LengthPrefixByteCount..]);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Writes a protobuf message as a length-prefixed bridge frame.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="stream">Target stream.</param>
    /// <param name="message">Message to write.</param>
    /// <param name="cancellationToken">Cancellation token.</param>
    /// <returns>A task representing the write.</returns>
    public static async ValueTask WriteAsync(
        Stream stream,
        IMessage message,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(stream);
        var frame = Encode(message);
        await stream.WriteAsync(frame, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Reads one length-prefixed protobuf bridge frame from a stream.
    /// </summary>
    /// <typeparam name="T">Message type.</typeparam>
    /// <param name="stream">Source stream.</param>
    /// <param name="parser">Message parser.</param>
    /// <param name="maxFrameLength">Maximum accepted payload size.</param>
    /// <param name="cancellationToken">Cancellation token.</param>
    /// <returns>Decoded protobuf message.</returns>
    public static async ValueTask<T> ReadAsync<T>(
        Stream stream,
        MessageParser<T> parser,
        int maxFrameLength = DefaultMaxFrameLength,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        where T : IMessage<T>
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(stream);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(parser);

        var lengthBuffer = new byte[LengthPrefixByteCount];
        await ReadExactlyAsync(stream, lengthBuffer, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
        var length = BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt32BigEndian(lengthBuffer);
        ValidateLength(length, maxFrameLength);

        var payload = new byte[length];
        await ReadExactlyAsync(stream, payload, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
        return parser.ParseFrom(payload);
    }

    private static async ValueTask ReadExactlyAsync(
        Stream stream,
        byte[] buffer,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var offset = 0;
        while (offset < buffer.Length)
        {
            var read = await stream.ReadAsync(
                buffer.AsMemory(offset, buffer.Length - offset),
                cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
            if (read == 0)
            {
                throw new EndOfStreamException("Bridge stream ended before a complete frame was read.");
            }

            offset += read;
        }
    }

    private static void ValidateLength(int length, int maxFrameLength)
    {
        if (maxFrameLength < 1)
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(maxFrameLength), "Maximum frame length must be positive.");
        }

        if (length < 0)
        {
            throw new InvalidDataException("Bridge frame length was negative.");
        }

        if (length > maxFrameLength)
        {
            throw new InvalidDataException(
                $"Bridge frame length {length} exceeds the maximum allowed length {maxFrameLength}.");
        }
    }
}

END ARTIFACT

BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT arc-upstream/19792a52a13a3df342f90bab6b246b547e673919/src/Avalonia.RemoteControl.Protocol/Protos/remote_control.proto SHA256 d22f19ce8d170f92c4e2f27bc2b6e4705337aa3d5fa6aed86d8e7eba2f95e79e
syntax = "proto3";

option csharp_namespace = "Avalonia.RemoteControl.Protocol.V1";

package avalonia.remotecontrol.v1;

service RemoteControl {
  rpc GetCapabilities(GetCapabilitiesRequest) returns (GetCapabilitiesResponse);
  rpc GetSnapshot(GetSnapshotRequest) returns (TreeSnapshot);
  rpc WatchTree(WatchTreeRequest) returns (stream TreeUpdate);
  rpc WatchFrames(WatchFramesRequest) returns (stream FrameUpdate);
  rpc InvokeClick(InvokeClickRequest) returns (CommandResult);
  rpc InvokeFocus(InvokeFocusRequest) returns (CommandResult);
  rpc SetProperty(SetPropertyRequest) returns (CommandResult);
  rpc SendInput(SendInputRequest) returns (CommandResult);
  rpc WatchLogs(WatchLogsRequest) returns (stream LogEntry);
}

message GetCapabilitiesRequest {
}

message GetCapabilitiesResponse {
  string protocol_version = 1;
  bool supports_tree_snapshots = 2;
  bool supports_tree_streaming = 3;
  bool supports_click_invocation = 4;
  bool supports_property_mutation = 5;
  bool supports_log_streaming = 6;
  bool supports_frame_streaming = 7;
  bool supports_remote_input = 8;
  string authenticated_client_identity = 9;
}

message GetSnapshotRequest {
}

message WatchTreeRequest {
}

message TreeSnapshot {
  uint64 sequence = 1;
  repeated TreeNode nodes = 2;
}

message TreeUpdate {
  uint64 sequence = 1;
  TreeSnapshot snapshot = 2;
  string reason = 3;
}

message TreeNode {
  string id = 1;
  string parent_id = 2;
  string type_name = 3;
  string name = 4;
  string automation_id = 5;
  Rect bounds = 6;
  bool is_visible = 7;
  bool is_enabled = 8;
  bool is_focused = 9;
  repeated PropertyValue properties = 10;
  repeated string classes = 11;
  string automation_name = 12;
  Rect absolute_bounds = 13;
}

message Rect {
  double x = 1;
  double y = 2;
  double width = 3;
  double height = 4;
}

message PropertyValue {
  string name = 1;
  string declaring_type = 2;
  string value = 3;
  string value_type = 4;
  bool can_write = 5;
  bool is_redacted = 6;
  bool is_enum = 7;
  repeated string enum_values = 8;
}

message InvokeClickRequest {
  string node_id = 1;
}

message InvokeFocusRequest {
  string node_id = 1;
}

message SetPropertyRequest {
  string node_id = 1;
  string property_name = 2;
  string value = 3;
}

message WatchFramesRequest {
  uint32 max_frames_per_second = 1;
}

message FrameUpdate {
  uint64 sequence = 1;
  bytes png = 2;
  int32 pixel_width = 3;
  int32 pixel_height = 4;
  double root_width = 5;
  double root_height = 6;
  double render_scale = 7;
  string timestamp_utc = 8;
}

message SendInputRequest {
  repeated RemoteInputEvent events = 1;
}

message RemoteInputEvent {
  RemoteInputKind kind = 1;
  double x = 2;
  double y = 3;
  RemoteMouseButton button = 4;
  double delta_x = 5;
  double delta_y = 6;
  string key = 7;
  string text = 8;
  uint64 timestamp = 9;
}

enum RemoteInputKind {
  REMOTE_INPUT_KIND_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
  REMOTE_INPUT_KIND_POINTER_MOVE = 1;
  REMOTE_INPUT_KIND_POINTER_PRESS = 2;
  REMOTE_INPUT_KIND_POINTER_RELEASE = 3;
  REMOTE_INPUT_KIND_WHEEL = 4;
  REMOTE_INPUT_KIND_KEY_DOWN = 5;
  REMOTE_INPUT_KIND_KEY_UP = 6;
  REMOTE_INPUT_KIND_TEXT = 7;
}

enum RemoteMouseButton {
  REMOTE_MOUSE_BUTTON_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
  REMOTE_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT = 1;
  REMOTE_MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT = 2;
  REMOTE_MOUSE_BUTTON_MIDDLE = 3;
}

message CommandResult {
  bool succeeded = 1;
  string message = 2;
}

message WatchLogsRequest {
  string minimum_level = 1;
  string category_prefix = 2;
}

message LogEntry {
  uint64 sequence = 1;
  string timestamp_utc = 2;
  string level = 3;
  string category = 4;
  int32 event_id = 5;
  string message = 6;
  string exception_summary = 7;
  uint64 dropped_count = 8;
  string structured_state = 9;
  string scope_summary = 10;
}

enum BridgeMethod {
  BRIDGE_METHOD_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
  BRIDGE_METHOD_GET_CAPABILITIES = 1;
  BRIDGE_METHOD_GET_SNAPSHOT = 2;
  BRIDGE_METHOD_WATCH_TREE = 3;
  BRIDGE_METHOD_INVOKE_CLICK = 4;
  BRIDGE_METHOD_INVOKE_FOCUS = 5;
  BRIDGE_METHOD_SET_PROPERTY = 6;
  BRIDGE_METHOD_WATCH_LOGS = 7;
  BRIDGE_METHOD_CANCEL = 8;
  BRIDGE_METHOD_WATCH_FRAMES = 9;
  BRIDGE_METHOD_SEND_INPUT = 10;
}

enum BridgeStatus {
  BRIDGE_STATUS_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
  BRIDGE_STATUS_OK = 1;
  BRIDGE_STATUS_ERROR = 2;
  BRIDGE_STATUS_UNAUTHENTICATED = 3;
  BRIDGE_STATUS_UNAUTHORIZED = 4;
  BRIDGE_STATUS_UNSUPPORTED = 5;
  BRIDGE_STATUS_CANCELLED = 6;
}

message BridgeRequest {
  string protocol_version = 1;
  string request_id = 2;
  BridgeMethod method = 3;
  string authorization = 4;
  bytes payload = 5;
}

message BridgeResponse {
  string protocol_version = 1;
  string request_id = 2;
  BridgeStatus status = 3;
  bytes payload = 4;
  string error_message = 5;
  bool end_of_stream = 6;
}
